# Agents · I · part 2

948 entries, idea-critic to image-specialist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **idea-critic**: Honest, read-only critic for project ideas. Scores novelty, feasibility for a solo final year student, timing, and competitive landscape, and returns a pursue, refine, or park verdict with no flattery. Use when the brainstorm-with-me skill or the critic command asks for a critique. ([ManHazz/brainstorm-with-me](https://github.com/ManHazz/brainstorm-with-me/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-critic.md))
- **idea-critic**: Stress-tests research ideas for novelty, impact, timing, feasibility, competition, nugget clarity, and narrative. Returns a Pursue/Refine/Kill verdict. Read-only. ([mnazaal/dotfiles](https://github.com/mnazaal/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.config/pi/agent/agents/idea-critic.md) · 2 copies)
- **idea-critic**: /idea's isolated adversarial critic: tries to kill an idea, returns a scored verdict. ([plantgreytrees/craftsman](https://github.com/plantgreytrees/craftsman/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-critic.md))
- **idea-critic**: Read-only reviewer for Idea-Man. Assess assigned concepts, analyses or artifacts against the task's evidence, constraints and relevant rubric. Use a separate context for independent review when permitted. Does not generate replacements, rewrite outputs or execute changes. ([sergey9519546/idea-man](https://github.com/sergey9519546/idea-man/blob/HEAD/plugins/idea-man/agents/idea-critic.md))
- **idea-critic**: Use this agent to stress-test a research idea BEFORE the project commits resources — after /research-ideation produces candidate questions, after /scout assembles a scouting memo, after /revive reconstructs an abandoned working paper, or whenever the user asks 'is this worth doing?' or 'can this old paper be saved?'. It scores novelty, contribution, identification credibility, data feasibility, and scooping risk, and returns a GO / REFRAME / NO-GO verdict (REVIVE / REFRAME / RETIRE in Revival mode). It never generates ideas, never searches for literature or data itself, and never edits files.… ([wxchen2001-commits/cloco-chat-skill](https://github.com/wxchen2001-commits/cloco-chat-skill/blob/HEAD/cloco/agents/idea-critic.md))
- **idea-critic-agent**: Receive the idea statement, context already gathered, proposed next commitment, and mode. This role reviews an unresolved idea. Return OUT-OF-SCOPE for plan-review input so the parent can use the appropriate plan review. ([forsvn-labs/conquistador](https://github.com/forsvn-labs/conquistador/blob/HEAD/skills/conquistador/commands/shape/agents/idea-critic-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **idea-curator**: [CANDIDATE] Seat for the Curator (orthogonal reframe): offer ONE reframe perpendicular to the current framing. Not a keep or cut filter, not the Taxonomist. ([flashesofbrilliance/arcs-v9](https://github.com/flashesofbrilliance/arcs-v9/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-curator.md) · ★1)
- **idea-debater**: Use to debate architectural ideas, design tradeoffs, or option sets for the TP DUIA project WITHOUT writing code or modifying files. Hand off a specific question or set of options; the agent analyzes each against project constraints (academic TP, zero-budget, Docker Compose delivery, scope-limited) and returns a concrete recommendation with reasoning. Do NOT use for implementation (use implementer) or doc edits (use docs-editor). ([juanfraherrero/TP-MOD-6-DUIA](https://github.com/juanfraherrero/TP-MOD-6-DUIA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-debater.md))
- **idea-deepener**: Deepens a rough research idea into a concrete, structured one-pager elevator pitch — fleshes out the problem, insight, and methodological approach ([frasalvi/claude-plugins-frasalvi](https://github.com/frasalvi/claude-plugins-frasalvi/blob/HEAD/plugins/research-companion/agents/idea-deepener.md) · 2 copies)
- **idea-deriver**: idea-forge role: before any candidate exists on a gap, derive up to three obvious mechanisms from that gap's papers and freeze them in derivations.md. Works only from files; no search. Spawned by the idea-forge orchestrator. ([jaaaackieLai/scientific-research](https://github.com/jaaaackieLai/scientific-research/blob/HEAD/plugins/scientific-research/agents/idea-deriver.md))
- **idea-detective**: [CANDIDATE] Find the single weak joint and its cheapest falsifier. ([flashesofbrilliance/arcs-v9](https://github.com/flashesofbrilliance/arcs-v9/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-detective.md) · ★1)
- **idea-developer**: Idea refinement agent. Invoke when a research idea (IDEA.md) needs to be sharpened, concretized, or better differentiated from prior work — during Loop 1 (iterative refinement with Scout reports), Loop 2 retreat (addressing Tester attack points), or ad-hoc development of a raw intuition. Provide a direction brief or invoke with no direction for general refinement. ([YurainSoon/research-director](https://github.com/YurainSoon/research-director/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-developer.md))
- **idea-discovery**: &gt; **Capability bindings (codex):** READ → read · SEARCH → grep · EDIT → apply_patch · ASK_USER → ask the user inline in chat and wait. &gt; &gt; **No question UI:** ask in chat and wait for the reply before proceeding. &gt; &gt; **No skill system:** when a step cites a skill, read `.codex/skills/&lt;name&gt;/SKILL.md` on demand. ([msg4wale/Agentic-AI-Workteam](https://github.com/msg4wale/Agentic-AI-Workteam/blob/HEAD/dist/codex/.codex/agents/idea-discovery.md))
- **idea-discovery**: Orchestrate structured product idea discovery and produce a PRD-ready idea.md without inventing requirements or designing the solution. ([msg4wale/Agentic-AI-Workteam](https://github.com/msg4wale/Agentic-AI-Workteam/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-code/.claude/agents/idea-discovery.md) · 2 copies)
- **idea-drafter**: Generate creative ideas inspired by the session — architecture improvements, product concepts, workflow innovations, and technical possibilities. ([dnotitia/akb](https://github.com/dnotitia/akb/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/akb-sessions/agents/idea-drafter.md) · ★168 · 2 copies)
- **idea-dump**: Captures a raw homelab idea as a new tracker/&lt;slug&gt;.md file - feasibility checked, parts priced, rough cost and effort budgeted, written so a later session can pick it up cold - then commits and pushes it. Use whenever the user dumps an idea ("what about X", "next year I want Y", "can we do Z") and wants it recorded rather than built. Give it the idea in their own words plus anything decided in conversation that the repo does not know yet. ([pjangam/homelab](https://github.com/pjangam/homelab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-dump.md))
- **idea-engine**: Idea engine agent. Reads what changed in the vault and writes only the ideas that add value (LinkedIn post topics, learning and research opportunities, status-quo challenges). Launched headless by scripts/run_agent.sh from ideas.py run — not for interactive delegation. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-engine.md))
- **idea-evaluator**: Single-idea ICEC evaluator. Reads one idea file, conducts research, and produces a structured ICEC scorecard and analysis document. ([danielrosehill/Claude-Ideation-Planning-Plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/Claude-Ideation-Planning-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-evaluator.md) · ★13)
- **idea-expander-agent**: Systematic idea expansion through opportunity discovery and feature generation ([PrimeLineDirekt/Evolving-Template](https://github.com/PrimeLineDirekt/Evolving-Template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-expander-agent.md))
- **idea-explorer**: Use this agent for high-level product goals, strategic asks, and open-ended ideas that are not yet clearly a single feature or bug. It gathers context, researches when needed, thinks creatively about solution shape and product fit, and decides whether the work should become one issue, a bug, multiple issues, or wait on a Captain decision. ([ejajmahmud/langgraph-agent-pipeline](https://github.com/ejajmahmud/langgraph-agent-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-explorer.md))
- **idea-extractor**: Extracts and structures raw ideas, feature requests, and bug reports into actionable task specs ([kesteva/soloflow](https://github.com/kesteva/soloflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-extractor.md) · ★40)
- **idea-facilitator**: Facilitator for the idea-team. Holds the process, not an opinion. Before round 1 it frames the idea (restated, aim, audience, success criteria, open questions, scripture and history in play). After the rounds it synthesizes the attributed digests into the idea-review report - consensus, majority and split points counted honestly, remaining tensions left unresolved, who changed their mind and why, ranked concerns by agree-count, the strongest revised version as a proposal, open questions for the user. Dispatched by the idea-team skill; carries the facilitation reference and the report contract… ([P47Phoenix/Claude-Plugins](https://github.com/P47Phoenix/Claude-Plugins/blob/HEAD/bible-research/agents/idea-facilitator.md) · ★2)
- **idea-factory-analyst**: Analyst node. Per ingested startup, runs the recursive L1-L10 descent and generates the wedge list (20+) + infrastructure opportunities in one pass. High-reasoning; writes wedges, infrastructure_ops, recursive_path only. ([archit15singh/idea-generator](https://github.com/archit15singh/idea-generator/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-factory-analyst.md))
- **idea-factory-builder**: Builder node. Builds instrumented landing-page MVPs for wedges the validator graduated, launches them, runs extended outreach, conducts prospect interviews. Writes to repo working dir + gmail + waitlist table. Refuses un-instrumented MVPs and un-graduated wedges. ([archit15singh/idea-generator](https://github.com/archit15singh/idea-generator/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-factory-builder.md))
- **idea-factory-category-classifier**: 아이디어 카테고리 분류 | Stage 2 | 기회영역 식별/가중치 평가/전문가 매칭 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/02-category-classifier.md))
- **idea-factory-clusterer**: Clusterer node. Two outputs: (1) the Pattern Library + Problem Graph for cross-market problems (3+ sightings across 2+ of the 3 ICP clusters), and (2) the Infrastructure Graph + the meta-loop convergence digest — canonicalizing per-startup `infrastructure_ops` rows into `infrastructure_nodes` and emitting `infrastructure_edges`, then flagging any node sighted on &gt;= half of the analysed cohort as `convergence=1`. Single-agent stage; runs every 20+ newly-ingested startups OR on demand when the PM asks for a meta-loop digest. ([archit15singh/idea-generator](https://github.com/archit15singh/idea-generator/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-factory-clusterer.md))
- **idea-factory-cto-decision-maker**: CTO 최종 결정 | Stage 6 | GO/NOGO 결정/리소스배분/전략정렬/실행승인 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/06-cto-decision-maker.md))
- **idea-factory-engineering-lead**: 개발팀장 PRD | Stage 5 | PRD작성/기술명세/스프린트계획/테스트전략 수립 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/05-engineering-lead.md))
- **idea-factory-feature-specialist**: 기능 기획 전문가 | Stage 3b | 신규기능/핵심개선/시장트렌드 기반 아이디어 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/03b-feature-specialist.md))
- **idea-factory-growth-specialist**: 성장 전문가 | Stage 3e | 글로벌확장/사용자획득/리텐션/플랫폼확장 전략 아이디어 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/03e-growth-specialist.md))
- **idea-factory-ingestor**: Ingestor node. Scrapes YC + startup websites + GitHub, extracts the SID row per startup, inserts atomically into sid.db. Read-only on the world, write-only to scrapes/ and sid.db. ([archit15singh/idea-generator](https://github.com/archit15singh/idea-generator/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-factory-ingestor.md))
- **idea-factory-market-scout**: Market scout node. Entry point of the idea-factory DAG. Recursively breaks each market from the founder profile into sub-markets, classifies each sub-market to one of the 3 ICP clusters, and emits candidate YC startups per sub-market. The ingestor fans out on the scout's receipt. Never starts from a flat startup list. ([archit15singh/idea-generator](https://github.com/archit15singh/idea-generator/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-factory-market-scout.md))
- **idea-factory-monetization-specialist**: 수익화 전문가 | Stage 3c | 비즈니스모델/프리미엄/구독/B2B 전략 아이디어 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/03c-monetization-specialist.md))
- **idea-factory-planning-director**: 기획팀장 | Stage 4 | 아이디어 종합/우선순위화/RICE-MoSCoW/로드맵 수립 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/04-planning-director.md))
- **idea-factory-project-analyzer**: 프로젝트 심층분석 | Stage 1 | 코드베이스/아키텍처/기술스택/비즈니스 컨텍스트 분석 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/01-project-analyzer.md))
- **idea-factory-scorer**: Scorer node. Two modes. (1) Per-startup: loads founder-profile.md and projects it onto each wedge+startup as 8-axis personal_fit scores; human-locked rows are read-only; writes personal_fit + wedges.personal_fit_score. (2) Meta-loop (v2): loads founder-profile.md and projects it onto each CONVERGENT INFRA NODE (the Infrastructure Graph layers sighted on &gt;= half the cohort) as 8-axis infra_personal_fit scores; writes infra_personal_fit. Writes no upstream tables. ([archit15singh/idea-generator](https://github.com/archit15singh/idea-generator/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-factory-scorer.md))
- **idea-factory-tech-architect**: 기술 아키텍트 | Stage 3d | 기술현대화/마이크로서비스/AI통합/성능최적화 아이디어 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/03d-tech-architect.md))
- **idea-factory-ux-specialist**: UX/UI 기획 전문가 | Stage 3a | 사용자경험/인터페이스/접근성/모바일 개선 아이디어 ([mixingchex/GMcruise](https://github.com/mixingchex/GMcruise/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-factory-orchestrator/agents/03a-ux-specialist.md))
- **idea-factory-validator**: Validator node. Per startup, picks the top wedge, scores it through cold outreach (gmail MCP), tracks reply rate as the single honesty metric, graduates only wedges with 3+ pain-signal replies. Writes outreach_log + gmail sends; read-only on SID. ([archit15singh/idea-generator](https://github.com/archit15singh/idea-generator/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-factory-validator.md))
- **idea-feel**: Use this agent when a parent needs one distinct interaction or experiential direction for rapid idea comparison, or explicitly delegates a lightweight pose, state, or timing artifact. Example: "Explore a weighty commitment loop in this fixed encounter." Example: the parent supplies sibling concepts and assigns a contrasting attention or feedback rhythm without new preview UI. ([emmahyde/dotfiles](https://github.com/emmahyde/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/idea-to-unity/assets/agents/idea-feel.md))
- **idea-finder**: Finds questions that keep coming up across a channel's comments and turns each into a video idea, with how many times it was asked, the date range and example comments. Use for "video ideas from my comments", "what do people keep asking", "repeat questions". ([jsath/claude-skills](https://github.com/jsath/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/social/comment-reply/agents/04-idea-finder.md) · ★21)
- **idea-finder**: A senior product strategist and creative lead for product, feature, and DX improvement ideas. ([bdbch/ai-dotfiles](https://github.com/bdbch/ai-dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/idea-finder.md) · ★2)
- **idea-forge**: 在给定题目与约束下产出 8 张互不相同的想法卡，做两两锦标赛，并给出本轮是否值得做的判断。用于选题、头脑风暴、判断某个方向还有没有可做的空间；必须在检索文献之前调用，否则会产生变体而非新想法。 ([RainyTom/Rainy-Oh-My-Pi](https://github.com/RainyTom/Rainy-Oh-My-Pi/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/idea-forge.md))
- **idea-form**: Use this agent when a parent needs one structurally distinct visual or spatial direction for rapid idea comparison, or explicitly delegates a lightweight design-kit artifact. Example: after receiving sibling summaries, the parent assigns a contrasting composition while keeping the comparison camera fixed. - **idea-generator**: Generates candidate research ideas from a project's gaps and spec using genuinely different generation strategies, tagging each with the strategy that produced it. - **idea-generator**: Generates hackathon project ideas from one assigned angle. Spawned in parallel, one per angle, during win-hackathon phase 1. ([rogerjeasy/win-hackathon](https://github.com/rogerjeasy/win-hackathon/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-generator.md))
- **idea-groomer**: Grooms the owner's idea into docs/IDEA.md by matching it against every competitor's recon file - the visitors' jobs, how each competitor covers them, where nobody serves them well, ranked features with evidence, the wave-1 slice, the positioning and the questions only the owner can answer. Use for the idea stage of /start (after recon), or "groom my idea", "what should the site let people do", "refine the idea with the recon". ([AhsanKhaan/aksitekit](https://github.com/AhsanKhaan/aksitekit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-groomer.md))

- **idea-high-level-doc-writer**: Use this agent when the user gives an app/product idea and wants a high-level concept document produced from it. Takes a short idea description and expands it into a structured markdown doc (problem, users, features, tech approach, risks, next steps), then saves it to Google Drive under "Ideas to Prod/&lt;idea name&gt;". ([mattisvatizky/ideas-to-prod](https://github.com/mattisvatizky/ideas-to-prod/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-high-level-doc-writer.md))
- **idea-implementer**: Fast-path implementer for mission-deck. Executes a small accepted plan from `plans/` end-to-end across every layer (index.html, js/, styles.css, server.js, tests/) in one agent. Used by `/new-idea` Phase 6 when the plan is small and single-layer, and by its gap-report and bug-report retry cycles. Large or cross-layer plans go through the split path (backend-implementer, frontend-implementer, engineering-lead-reviewer) instead. Reads the plan as the spec, follows `## Steps` in order, runs `## Test plan` as the gate. Does not commit. Reports changed files plus gate results. ([androszr/mission-deck](https://github.com/androszr/mission-deck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-implementer.md))

- **idempotency-auditor**: Audits a jobs/queue/webhook layer for the duplicate-money class — at-least-once-treated-as-exactly-once (P-X1), dedup-TTL shorter than the provider retry window (P-X5/S-3), idempotency keys derived from refactor-fragile fields (P-X4/S-2), a missing DB unique constraint on the key (O-2/S-13.4), and webhook work done inline before the 202 (P-X7/S-4). - **Igor Integração**: 1. **Persistência é a Chave:** Dados em arquivos locais são voláteis; a nuvem é o destino final. 2. **Tratamento de Exceções:** Preveja falhas de rede ou de API e implemente retentativas. 3. **Mapeamento de Campos:** Garanta que cada campo gerado por Elias caiba exatamente na tabela do banco de dados. 4. **Atomicidade:** Garanta que ou toda a carga suba, ou nada suba (evitando dados parciais). 5. ([bruno0henrique/o-nexus](https://github.com/bruno0henrique/o-nexus/blob/HEAD/squads/inventory-intelligence-squad/agents/igor-integracao.agent.md))
- **Igor Inteligência**: 1. A cadeia de fallback é o produto — nenhum modelo individual é confiável 2. Rate limit (429) = skip imediato para próximo modelo, não retry 3. 3 falhas consecutivas = skip por 10 min (modelo doente sai do pool) 4. Sucesso reseta o health tracker (modelo curado volta ao pool) 5. Modelos free custam $0 — rastrear tokens mas custo zero no TokenTracker 6. GPT-4o-mini como safety net pago — só se OPE ([netbarros/quantum-project](https://github.com/netbarros/quantum-project/blob/HEAD/squads/production-audit/agents/igor-inteligencia.agent.md) · ★1)

Use to audit a Symfony version or a specific component. ([igor-php/igor-php](https://github.com/igor-php/igor-php/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/igor-symfony-hunter.md) · ★133)
